<div class="otQkpb" data-animation-nesting="" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-processed="true" data-sae="" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 600; margin: 24px 0px 12px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">Part 4: Crucial Talking Points for Your Meeting with the Manager<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></div>
<div class="n6owBd awi2gc" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-hveid="CAAIGxAA" data-complete="true" data-processed="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 12px 0px 16px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">When you sit down with the manager, speak like a project director, not a borrower. Highlight these three points:<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></div>
<ol class="IaGLZe VimKh" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-processed="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 12px 0px 16px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">
<li class="Z1qcYe" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-hveid="CAAIHBAA" data-complete="true" data-sae="" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px 0px 12px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><span class="iNqyIf" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><strong class="rQesXe MPyX" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">Zero Corporate Competition:<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></strong> Explain that unlike Gulkand (where Patanjali dominates), the <strong class="rQesXe MPyX" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">Natural Luffa Scrubber<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></strong> sector is highly unorganized. People are looking for eco-friendly alternatives to plastic loofahs on Amazon, and you are capturing a clean, brand-free market.<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></span><!--TgQPHd|||[]--></li>
<li class="Z1qcYe" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-hveid="CAAIHBAD" data-complete="true" data-sae="" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px 0px 12px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><span class="iNqyIf" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><strong class="rQesXe MPyX" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">Infrastructure as Security:<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></strong> Show her that ₹5.7 Lakhs out of the loan is going directly into constructing a workshed and a deep borewell on your family's own land. This permanently appreciates the value of the property, giving the bank massive structural security.<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></span><!--TgQPHd|||[]--></li>
<li class="Z1qcYe" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-hveid="CAAIHBAG" data-complete="true" data-sae="" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px 0px 12px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><span class="iNqyIf" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><strong class="rQesXe MPyX" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">High-Margin Year-Round Revenue:<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></strong> Emphasize that the hydraulic press handles Cow Dung Dhoop cups continuously, while the washing/drying bays handle Luffa fiber processing simultaneously. This ensures the unit never sits idle, generating steady daily cash flow to pay your workers' salaries and the bank EMI on time.<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></span><!--TgQPHd|||[]--></li>
<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></ol>
